Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in activity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 867 and the invariant I(A,B) = 359 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 867 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the activity structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 359. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
